Insight

3190 Real Forward Deployed Engineer Job Descriptions — The Archive

How to Become an FDE — Interview Kickstart · 2026-05-22 · 175 min read

Summary

This article is an archive of 3,190 Forward Deployed Engineer job descriptions gathered from company career boards and LinkedIn since May 2026, spanning 1,358 employers. Even the 146 postings since taken down are retained, since a filled role still shows the employer hires for it. Each entry links to the original posting and lists requirements, seniority level, disclosed pay bands, and an excerpt. The visible listings show Google Cloud as the dominant employer, with FDE roles across the US, Europe, Asia, and Latin America, mostly Generative AI and Applied AI focused, and US salary bands ranging roughly from $123K for entry-level to $365K for senior roles.

Key points

  • Archive of 3,190 FDE postings from 1,358 employers.
  • 146 removed postings kept as evidence of hiring.
  • Google Cloud dominates the listed FDE openings.
  • Roles span GenAI, Applied AI, and public sector.
  • US pay bands run from about $123K to $365K.

Full text

Primary sources

Most career advice about the Forward Deployed Engineer role is somebody's opinion. This is the postings themselves — 3,190 of them, from 1358 employers, collected from company careers boards and from LinkedIn since May 2026. 146 have since been taken down and are kept here anyway: a filled role still proves the employer hires for it.

Each entry links to the employer's original posting where it still exists. We show the requirements, the seniority, the pay band where it was disclosed, and an excerpt of the posting — not a copy of it.
